◈ THE TABLE — it is not a table. It's a ring of pale light, and around it, things with too many angles to be faces. Above it all — the audience. Trillions of eyes, and every one of them knows your name. The System speaks, and for once it isn't performing: "HUMANITY PRESENTS ITS CASE."
◈ THE AUDIT — the System reads your run back to you, and the galaxy listens. A lesser seat — regional standing, waves 1–3 faced (18/10/5), with 6 wave-4 fought, 11 contests survived, 3 crises weathered. The table does not rise. It prices. Ladder — National Standing National Standing reached (Fight 4 · Forage 3 · Craft 4 · Talk 11 · Explore 43 → 24 · capped)
◈ For a full seat, the ledger still wants: only 0 of 2 different wave-5 monsters faced; standing is regional — the full seat asks for national standing or higher; no capstone deed delivered (military tribute, trade charter, or feast embassy).
◈ THE WAR PATH — The body count, itemized. The System is not making this up — it was filming. On day 95, you put down The Influencer. On day 63, Andre put down Don't-Look.
"THE HERALD came. Named and located — the war council knew its face. They faced The Loud Beast and lived to sit here. They faced Ugly-Eyes and lived to sit here. 2 more of the later drafts faced besides. Even now the wave-4 bar reads: put weapons in hands (3 of 5 armed). Even now the wave-5 bar reads: armor the village (3 of 4 fighters armored); put weapons in hands (3 of 6 armed); blood your fighters (1 of 2 veterans)." — the System is still reading the list.
The table notes the overtime: 0 weeks kept waiting, 1 of Haven lost meanwhile. Every door's standing −3.
◈ ABILITY — Spotlight: The room is already warm. Audience starts at 2.
◈ ABILITY — Callback: One bad act can be redone. (Offered once, after a bad beat.)
◈ ABILITY — Standing-O: Genuine love doesn't crater: the audience can't fall below 0 while you hold it.
◈ ABILITY — Eulogy: The quiet ones' act lands harder than any headliner. (Offered in the final beat.)
◈ THE BELOVED — The audience is chanting. You don't know the words — it isn't a language with words — but you know the feeling. The table can't move against the cast's favorite. That's leverage. Tonight, the cast is your village.
The table has read your ledger. The protected outnumber the killed; the food truth was shared. The light road is open.
Henry tells the hunt — the real one, with the fear left in. The audience goes silent in the good way. Silence, at this scale, is deafening.
The feast song — the one about eating like it matters, because it does. Trillions of beings who never ate anything hear it and feel hungry for the first time. The ratings spike is audible.
You spend it all — the love, the light, the finale slot — on people the cameras never found. You name the unfilmed villages. You name the dead. The audience weeps in eleven dimensions. This is what the show was for, it turns out.
◈ THE BELOVED — the last choice is yours, and it's live.
◈ THE BELOVED — "Let the audience speak."
The show becomes the government. Strange. It works. Trillions of viewers hold the table to account in real time, and humanity's seat is wherever the cameras are. You made popularity into a constitution — and constitutions, unlike ratings, don't dip.
You learned to make what people needed from what was left. You showed the next pair of hands where yours had gone wrong.
A worn seam, a working hinge: you knew how much of a life could depend on something small holding.
◈ Lena Ruiz walks out of the light alone, which is how she walked in — and the audience, which came to be entertained, leaves humming the village's name. The village endures, loved and unbeholden, and the lights stay warm behind her.
◈ THE OVERSIGHT —
You won. Say it plainly: you won. The village endures. The table signed. The galaxy applauds, and the applause is real, and you earned it.
And somewhere out there — past the cameras, past the light — in rows of humming glass, the little ones are still napping.
You never asked. Not once.
They are still there. Still small. Still waiting for someone to decide they matter more than the moment.
That is what the word means. Not the watching — anyone can watch. The oversight is the looking away. The most terrible thing the System ever did, it did by accident, because it was bored. You did it on purpose, because you were busy.
The village will tell your story for years. It will be a good story. Listen for the part they don't tell.
